-- ADAPT optimized build (.adapt)
-- Performance changes:
--   * Removed injected unconditional console spam.
--   * Reduced anti-lag scan burst size to avoid frame-time spikes.
--   * Yielded large visual scans more frequently.
--   * Throttled idle auto-steal polling slightly.

-- Layout:
--   1. Bootstrap and shared runtime
--   2. Intro audio, font helpers, and the steal-bar HUD
--   3. Plot synchronization, stealing, carry, and movement controllers
--   4. Character physics, ragdoll recovery, anti-lag, and visual effects
--   5. Sky presets, avatar skins, and settings persistence
--   6. Targeting, bat controllers, counters, and ESP
--   7. Main interface, settings widgets, mobile controls, and Safe Mode
--   8. Save/reset UI and animation-pack hooks
--
-- The original build is kept as one entry-point script because its feature blocks
-- share local state and upvalues. Section labels below are comments only.

-- Adapt Volt (Luarmor auth removed)
local colors = nil
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
-- Main entry point; feature blocks share state through outer locals.

            adaptIntroSongIndex = #introSongs

            playIntroMusic = function(arg)
              if #introSongs ~= 0 then
                if type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(getcustomasset) ~= "function" then
                  warn("[Adapt] Intro music is unavailable: this executor does not support custom assets.")
                  return
                end

                task.spawn(function()
                  local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    local clamp = math.clamp
                    local floor2 = math.floor
                    local num = tonumber(_G._AdaptIntroSongIndex) or 1
                    local n34 = #introSongs
                    local v79 = introSongs[clamp(floor2(num), 1, n34)]
                    local str9 = "AdaptIntroMusic_" .. (v79.cacheFile or v79.url:match("/([^/]+)$") or "song.mp3"):gsub("[^%w%._%-]", "_")

                    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(str9) then
                      writefile(str9, game:HttpGet(v79.url))
                    end

                    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
                    sound.Name = "AdaptIntroMusic"
                    sound.SoundId = getcustomasset(str9)
                    sound.Volume = 0.7
                    sound.Looped = false
                    sound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
                    sound.TimePosition = tonumber(v79.startTime) or 0
                    sound:Play()
                    if arg and arg(sound) == false then
                      sound:Destroy()
                      return
                    end
                    task.wait(6)
                    if not sound.Parent then
                      return
                    end
                    local tween2 = TweenService:Create(sound, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Volume = 0 })
                    tween2:Play()
                    tween2.Completed:Wait()
                    sound:Destroy()
                  end)

                  if not ok then
                    warn("[Adapt] Could not play intro music:", result)
                  end
                end)

                return
              end

              do
                return
              end
            end

            playChainSpearAudio = function(arg)
              local flag17 = type(writefile) ~= "function"
              local flag18

              if flag17 then
                flag18 = flag17
              else
                flag18 = type(getcustomasset) ~= "function"
              end

              if flag18 then
                warn("[Adapt] Chain-spear audio is unavailable: this executor does not support custom assets.")
                return
              end

              task.spawn(function()
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                  if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile("AdaptChainSpear_k19260.mp3") then
                    writefile("AdaptChainSpear_k19260.mp3", game:HttpGet("https://files.catbox.moe/k19260.mp3"))
                  end

                  local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")

                  for _, child in ipairs(SoundService:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Sound") and child.Name == "AdaptChainSpearSound" then
                      child:Destroy()
                    end
                  end

                  local instance = Instance.new("Sound")
                  instance.Name = "AdaptChainSpearSound"
                  instance.SoundId = getcustomasset("AdaptChainSpear_k19260.mp3")
                  instance.Volume = 0
                  instance.Looped = true
                  instance.Parent = SoundService
                  instance:Play()
                  if arg and arg(instance) == false then
                    instance:Destroy()
                    return
                  end
                  TweenService:Create(instance, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Volume = 1.25 }):Play()
                end)

                if not ok then
                  warn("[Adapt] Could not play chain-spear audio:", result)
                end
              end)
            end

            -- Font mode and text styling.
            font = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
            _G._AdaptFontMode = _G._AdaptFontMode == "PIXEL" and "PIXEL" or "DEFAULT"

            _G._AdaptApplyFontMode = function(arg)
              local function fn43(arg2)
                if not (arg2:IsA("TextLabel") or arg2:IsA("TextButton") or arg2:IsA("TextBox")) then
                  return
                end

                if arg2:GetAttribute("_AdaptForceGothamBlack") then
                  pcall(function()
                    arg2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                  end)

                  return
                end

                if _G._AdaptFontMode == "PIXEL" then
                  if not _G._AdaptBoldPixelFont then
                    pcall(function()
                      local font2 = Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arcade)
                      _G._AdaptBoldPixelFont = Font.new(font2.Family, Enum.FontWeight.Bold, font2.Style)
                    end)
                  end

                  if _G._AdaptBoldPixelFont then
                    pcall(function()
                      arg2.FontFace = _G._AdaptBoldPixelFont
                    end)
                  else
                    pcall(function()
                      arg2.Font = Enum.Font.Arcade
                    end)
                  end

                  if arg2:GetAttribute("_AdaptFontOriginalStrokeTransparency") == nil then
                    arg2:SetAttribute("_AdaptFontOriginalStrokeTransparency", arg2.TextStrokeTransparency)
                    arg2:SetAttribute("_AdaptFontOriginalStrokeColor", arg2.TextStrokeColor3)
                  end

                  pcall(function()
                    arg2.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
                  end)
                else
                  pcall(function()
                    arg2.FontFace = font
                  end)

                  local attribute = arg2:GetAttribute("_AdaptFontOriginalFont")
                  local attribute2 = arg2:GetAttribute("_AdaptFontOriginalStrokeColor")

                  if attribute ~= nil then
                    pcall(function()
                      arg2.TextStrokeTransparency = attribute
                      arg2.TextStrokeColor3 = attribute2
                    end)

                    arg2:SetAttribute("_AdaptFontOriginalStrokeTransparency", nil)
                    arg2:SetAttribute("_AdaptFontOriginalStrokeColor", nil)
                  end
                end
              end

              if arg then
                fn43(arg)

                for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
                  fn43(descendant)
                end

                return
              end

              local v79 = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")

              if v79 then
                for _, descendant in ipairs(v79:GetDescendants()) do
                  fn43(descendant)
                end
              end

              local character = localPlayer.Character

              if character then
                for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                  fn43(descendant)
                end
              end
            end

            do
              local flag17 = false

              loadCustomFont = function()
                if _G._AdaptVoltCompatibility then
                  return
                end

                if flag17 then
                  return
                end
                flag17 = true

                task.spawn(function()
                  pcall(function()
                    if not (isfile and writefile and getcustomasset) then
                      return
                    end

                    if isfile("starborn.ttf") then
                      _G._AdaptApplyFontMode()
                      return
                    end
                    writefile("starborn.ttf", game:HttpGet("https://granny.anondrop.net/uploads/6c2505542959f371/Starborn.ttf"))

                    writefile(
                      "starborn.json",
                      game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({
                        name = "Starborn",
                        faces = {
                          {
                            name = "Regular",
                            weight = 0.7,
                            style = "normal",
                            assetId = getcustomasset("starborn.ttf"),
                          },
                        },
                      })
                    )

                    -- Optional custom-font loader.
                    font = Font.new(getcustomasset("starborn.json"))
                    task.wait(1)
                    _G._AdaptApplyFontMode()
                  end)
                end)
              end
            end
          end

          do
            _G._AdaptNormalPauseOptions = { 70, 80, 85, 90, 95, 100 }

            _G._AdaptNormalizeNormalPausePercent = function(arg)
              local n34 = tonumber(arg) or 75
              local v79 = 70
              local huge = math.huge

              for _, adaptNormalPauseOption in ipairs(_G._AdaptNormalPauseOptions) do
                local n35 = math.abs(n34 - adaptNormalPauseOption)

                if n35 < huge then
                  huge = n35
                  v79 = adaptNormalPauseOption
                end
              end

              return v79
            end

            normalStealSettings = {

              _G._AdaptAntiDropEnabled = false

              aimbot = false,
              aimSpd = 58,
              swing = false,
              desync = false,

              perfectHitRange = 175,

            -- Vanta Insta Reset transplant.
            local adaptInstaResetBusy = false

            local function adaptInstaReset()
              if adaptInstaResetBusy then
                return false
              end

              local character = localPlayer.Character
              local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
              local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
              if not character or not humanoid then
                return false
              end

              adaptInstaResetBusy = true

              local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
              local spawnConnection
              spawnConnection = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
                if spawnConnection then
                  pcall(function() spawnConnection:Disconnect() end)
                  spawnConnection = nil
                end
                task.spawn(function()
                  local newHumanoid = newCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    or newCharacter:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                  task.wait(0.1)
                  local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera or camera
                  if currentCamera and newHumanoid then
                    pcall(function()
                      currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                      currentCamera.CameraSubject = newHumanoid
                    end)
                  end
                  adaptInstaResetBusy = false
                end)
              end)

              pcall(function()
                humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
                humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                humanoid.Health = 0
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
              end)

              pcall(function()
                character:BreakJoints()
              end)

              if root and root.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                  root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -500, 0)
                  root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                  root.CFrame = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, -750, 0)
                end)
              end

              task.delay(1.5, function()
                if localPlayer.Character == character then
                  pcall(function()
                    humanoid.Health = 0
                    character:BreakJoints()
                    humanoid:Destroy()
                  end)
                end
                task.delay(2, function()
                  if adaptInstaResetBusy and localPlayer.Character == character then
                    adaptInstaResetBusy = false
                  end
                end)
              end)

              return true
            end

            _G._AdaptInstaReset = adaptInstaReset

            _G._AdaptTpBatSwingDelay = math.clamp(tonumber(_G._AdaptTpBatSwingDelay) or 0.007, 0.001, 1)
            _G._AdaptTpBatDistance = math.clamp(tonumber(_G._AdaptTpBatDistance) or 8, 0.5, 24)
            _G._AdaptTpBatV3Enabled = _G._AdaptTpBatV3Enabled == true

            if _G._AdaptTpBatCameraSteering == nil then
              _G._AdaptTpBatCameraSteering = true
            end

            -- Ryzen Hard Hit / Perfect Hit transplant.
            -- Hard Hit in the supplied Ryzen source is a state/range feature;
            -- its old ring renderer is intentionally a no-op, so no extra hit
            -- action is invented here. Perfect Hit keeps Ryzen's nearest-target
            -- scan and 45 ms swing cooldown.
            _G._AdaptRyzenHardHit = _G._AdaptRyzenHardHit or {
              enabled = false,
              range = 10,
            }
            _G._AdaptRyzenPerfectHit = _G._AdaptRyzenPerfectHit or {
              enabled = false,
              range = 175,
              cd = false,
              conn = nil,
            }

            local function adaptFindBatForPerfectHit()
              local character = localPlayer.Character
              if not character then return nil end
              local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
              if tool then
                return tool
              end
              return nil
            end

            local function adaptSwingBatForPerfectHit()
              local character = localPlayer.Character
              if not character then return end
              local bat = adaptFindBatForPerfectHit()
              if not bat then return end
              local ok = pcall(function()
                if _G._AdaptOshaBatCounter and _G._AdaptOshaBatCounter.hit then
                  _G._AdaptOshaBatCounter.hit(character)
                elseif bat.Activate then
                  bat:Activate()
                end
              end)
              return ok
            end

            local function adaptPerfectHitIsRagdoll()
              local character = localPlayer.Character
              local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
              if not hum then return false end
              local st = hum:GetState()
              return st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                or hum.PlatformStand == true
            end

            local function adaptPerfectHitGetTarget()
              local character = localPlayer.Character
              local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
              if not root then return nil end
              local nearest, shortest = nil, math.huge
              local range = tonumber(combatSettings.perfectHitRange) or 175
              for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= localPlayer and plr.Character then
                  local tr = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                  local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                  if tr and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local d = (tr.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                    if d <= range and d < shortest then
                      shortest = d
                      nearest = tr
                    end
                  end
                end
              end
              return nearest
            end

            local function adaptPerfectHitTryHit()
              local state = _G._AdaptRyzenPerfectHit
              if not state.enabled or state.cd then return end
              state.cd = true
              adaptSwingBatForPerfectHit()
              task.delay(0.045, function()
                state.cd = false
              end)
            end

            adaptStartPerfectHit = function()
              local state = _G._AdaptRyzenPerfectHit
              state.enabled = true
              combatSettings.perfectHit = true
              if state.conn then return end
              state.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not state.enabled then return end
                if not localPlayer.Character then return end
                if adaptPerfectHitIsRagdoll() then return end
                if adaptPerfectHitGetTarget() then
                  adaptPerfectHitTryHit()
                end
              end)
            end

            adaptStopPerfectHit = function()
              local state = _G._AdaptRyzenPerfectHit
              state.enabled = false
              combatSettings.perfectHit = false
              if state.conn then
                pcall(function() state.conn:Disconnect() end)
                state.conn = nil
              end
              state.cd = false
            end

            adaptSetHardHitRange = function(v)
              local n = tonumber(v)
              if not n then return end
              n = math.clamp(n, 1, 100)
              combatSettings.hardHitRange = n
              _G._AdaptRyzenHardHit.range = n
            end

            adaptSetPerfectHitRange = function(v)
              local n = tonumber(v)
              if not n then return end
              n = math.clamp(n, 10, 500)
              combatSettings.perfectHitRange = n
              _G._AdaptRyzenPerfectHit.range = n
            end

            _G._loadedOn_mirrorTP = false

            visualSettings = {
              antilag = false,
              sky = false,
              stretch = false,
              fov = false,
              fovVal = 120,
            }

            autoSaveSettings = { auto = true }

            -- Auto Carry additions.
            -- Auto Carry Speed follows the real carry/steal state and switches
            -- to the normal Carry speed while the player is holding a brainrot.
            -- Auto Carry on Enemy Base switches to Carry when near another
            -- player's base, using the same PlotSign/YourBase detection already
            -- used by Adapt's steal controller.
            _G._AdaptAutoCarrySpeedEnabled = _G._AdaptAutoCarrySpeedEnabled == true
            _G._AdaptAutoCarryEnemyBaseEnabled = _G._AdaptAutoCarryEnemyBaseEnabled == true
            _G._AdaptAutoCarryEnemyBaseRange = math.clamp(tonumber(_G._AdaptAutoCarryEnemyBaseRange) or 35, 5, 150)

            if _G._AdaptMobileBtnScale == nil then
              _G._AdaptMobileBtnScale = 1
            end

            if _G._AdaptUIScale == nil then
              _G._AdaptUIScale = 65
            end

            if _G._AdaptStealBarScale == nil then

                local v78 = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if not v78 or not plots then
                  return nil, nil, nil
                end
                local huge = math.huge
                local position = v78.Position
                local tbl38 = nil
                local v79 = nil

                for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                  if not fn40(child.Name) then
                    local animalPodiums = child:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

                    if animalPodiums then
                      for _, child2 in ipairs(animalPodiums:GetChildren()) do
                        local base = child2:FindFirstChild("Base")
                        base = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")

                        if base then
                          local magnitude = (base.Position - position).Magnitude

                          if magnitude < huge and magnitude <= normalStealSettings.StealRadius then
                            local promptAttachment = base:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")

                            if promptAttachment then
                              for _, child3 in ipairs(promptAttachment:GetChildren()) do
                                if child3:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tostring(child3.ActionText):find("Steal") then
                                  tbl38 = {
                                    name = child2.Name,
                                    plot = child.Name,
                                    slot = child2.Name,
                                    worldPosition = base.Position,
                                    uid = child.Name .. "_" .. child2.Name,
                                  }

                                  v79 = child3
                                  huge = magnitude
                                  break
                                end
                              end
                            end
                          end
                        end
                      end
                    end
                  end
                end

                return tbl38, v79, huge
              end

              local v78 = nil
              local v79 = nil

              local function fn49(arg, arg2, arg3)
                local v80 = tbl37[arg]
                if not v80 or not v80.ready or flag16 then
                  return false
                end

                if tick() - (_G._AdaptLastNormalSteal or 0) < 0.08 then
                  return false
                end

                if tween then
                  tween:Cancel()
                  tween = nil
                end

                v80.ready = false
                flag16 = true
                v78 = arg3
                v79 = arg
                n33 += 1
                local v81 = n33
                _G._AdaptLastNormalSteal = tick()
                now2 = tick()
                local now3 = nil
                local n34 = 0
                textLabel.Text = "0%"
                frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
                uiStroke.Color = color
                local v82 = _G._AdaptNormalizeNormalPausePercent(normalStealSettings.NormalStealPausePercent or 75)
                local n35 = 1.3 * v82 / 100

                local function fn50()
                  return v81 == n33 and flag16 and autoStealSettings.AutoSteal
                end

                local function fn51()
                  if connection2 then
                    connection2:Disconnect()
                    connection2 = nil
                  end

                  if v81 == n33 then
                    fn39()
                  end

                  v80.ready = true

                  if v81 == n33 then
                    flag16 = false
                    v78 = nil
                    v79 = nil
                  end
                end

                local function fn52()
                  local character = localPlayer.Character

                  if character then
                    character = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                  end

                  if not character then
                    return nil
                  end
                  local parent = arg.Parent
                  local worldPosition

                  while true do
                    local flag17 = parent and parent ~= workspace
                    worldPosition = nil

                    if flag17 then
                      if parent:IsA("Attachment") then
                        worldPosition = parent.WorldPosition
                        break
                      elseif parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                        worldPosition = parent.Position
                        break
                      else
                        parent = parent.Parent
                        continue
                      end
                    end

                    break
                  end

                  if not worldPosition then
                    return nil
                  end
                  local n36 = character.Position - worldPosition
                  return Vector3.new(n36.X, 0, n36.Z).Magnitude
                end

                if connection2 then
                  connection2:Disconnect()
                  connection2 = nil
                end

                local v83 = nil

                connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                  if not fn50() then
                    v83:Disconnect()

                    if connection2 == v83 then
                      connection2 = nil
                    end

                    return
                  end

                  local n36 = math.clamp(((now3 or tick()) - now2 - n34) / 1.3, 0, 1)
                  frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(n36, 1)
                  textLabel.Text = math.floor(n36 * 100) .. "%"
                end)

                task.spawn(function()
                  pcall(function()
                    arg.HoldDuration = 0
                  end)

                  for _, holdCallback in ipairs(v80.holdCallbacks) do
                    task.spawn(holdCallback)
                  end

                  while true do
                    if fn50() and tick() - now2 < n35 then
                      local v84 = fn52()
                      if not (not v84 or v84 > normalStealSettings.StealRadius) then
                        task.wait()
                        continue
                      end
                    end

                    break
                  end

                  if not fn50() then
                    if v81 == n33 then
                      v80.ready = true
                    end

                    return
                  end

                  local n36 = math.clamp(v82 / 100, 0, 1)
                  frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(n36, 1)
                  textLabel.Text = math.floor(n36 * 100) .. "%"
                  now3 = tick()
                  local n37 = math.max(2.99 - n35 - math.max(1.3 - n35, 0), 0.05)
                  local now4 = tick()

                  while fn50() do
                    if n37 <= tick() - now4 then
                      fn51()
                      return
                    end
                    local v84 = fn52()
                    if not v84 or v84 > normalStealSettings.StealRadius then
                      fn51()
                      return
                    end

                    if v84 <= 6 then
                      n34 += tick() - now3
                      now3 = nil
                      break
                    end

                    task.wait()
                  end

                  if not fn50() then
                    if v81 == n33 then
                      v80.ready = true
                    end

                    return
                  end

                  local now5 = tick()
                  local n38 = math.max(1.3 - n35, 0.05)

                  while fn50() and tick() - now5 < n38 do
                    task.wait()
                  end

                  if fn50() then
                    pcall(function()
                      for _, triggerCallback in ipairs(v80.triggerCallbacks) do
                        task.spawn(triggerCallback)
                      end

                      local stealAnimal = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("StealAnimal")

                      if stealAnimal and arg2 then
                        stealAnimal:FireServer(arg2)
                      end

                      if arg then
                        arg:Fire()
                      end
                    end)
                  end

                  fn51()
                end)

                return true
              end

              task.spawn(function()
                local v80 = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 12)
                if not v80 then
                  return
                end

                for _, child in ipairs(v80:GetChildren()) do

              task.spawn(function()

                  if plots then
